Model before rewriting:

letting a be {2,3}
letting b be {1,2}
find c: set (size 1) of int(1..3)

such that

and([(c subsetEq b),!((c subsetEq a));int(1..)])

c
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
c
new variables:
  find c#packed_1: int(0..7)
new constraints:
  sum([(c#packed_1 / 1) % 2,(c#packed_1 / 2) % 2,(c#packed_1 / 4) % 2;int(1..)]) = 1

  or([and([SATInt(Offset, [c#packed_1#offset_1,c#packed_1#offset_2,c#packed_1#offset_3;int(1..)] [0, 7]) >= 0,SATInt(Offset, [c#packed_1#offset_1,c#packed_1#offset_2,c#packed_1#offset_3;int(1..)] [0, 7]) <= 7;int(1..)]);int(1..)])

__0
